                                                                                  • इस दृष्टिकोण का उद्देश्य सार्वजनिक नियंत्रण और निजी दक्षता के बीच संतुलन बनाना है।
                                                                                    • \n\t
                                                                                  \n\t
                                                                                  • \n
                                                                                \n\nb5:Tfb6,\n

                                                                                Shortcut Trick\n

                                                                                Given: 25% A = 20% B = 10% C

                                                                                \n

                                                                                ⇒ 25A = 20B = 10C ⇒ 5A = 4B = 2C

                                                                                \n

                                                                                Find the LCM of the coefficients (5, 4, 2), which is 20.

                                                                                \n

                                                                                ⇒ A : B : C = (20 ÷ 5) : (20 ÷ 4) : (20 ÷ 2)

                                                                                \n

                                                                                ⇒ A : B : C = 4 : 5 : 10

                                                                                \n

                                                                                ∴ The correct answer is 4 : 5 : 10.

                                                                                \n

                                                                                Alternate Method

                                                                                \n

                                                                                Given:

                                                                                \n

                                                                                25% of A = 20% of B = 10% of C

                                                                                \n\n

                                                                                Calculation:

                                                                                \n

                                                                                Convert the given percentages into fractions:

                                                                                \n

                                                                                ⇒ (25 ÷ 100) × A = (20 ÷ 100) × B = (10 ÷ 100) × C

                                                                                \n

                                                                                ⇒ A / 4 = B / 5 = C / 10

                                                                                \n

                                                                                Let A / 4 = B / 5 = C / 10 = k (where k is a constant)

                                                                                \n

                                                                                ⇒ A = 4k

                                                                                \n

                                                                                ⇒ B = 5k

                                                                                \n

                                                                                ⇒ C = 10k

                                                                                \n

                                                                                ⇒ A : B : C = 4k : 5k : 10k

                                                                                \n

                                                                                ⇒ A : B : C = 4 : 5 : 10

                                                                                \n

                                                                                ∴ The correct answer is 4 : 5 : 10.

                                                                                \n

                                                                                Additional Information

                                                                                \n

                                                                                Compound Ratio

                                                                                \n

                                                                                The ratio obtained by multiplying antecedent to antecedent and consequent to consequent of two or more ratios is called a compound ratio. For a : b and c : d, the compound ratio is (a × c) : (b × d).

                                                                                \n

                                                                                Duplicate & Triplicate Ratio

                                                                                \n

                                                                                The duplicate ratio of a : b is a2 : b2, and the triplicate ratio of a : b is a3 : b3.

                                                                                \n

                                                                                Sub-duplicate Ratio

                                                                                \n\nb1:Td60,\n

                                                                                The correct answer is Exosphere - tropospheric clouds.

                                                                                \n\n

                                                                                Key Points

                                                                                \n\n
                                                                                  \n\t
                                                                                • The exosphere is the outermost layer of Earth's atmosphere, located above the thermosphere.
                                                                                  • \n\t
                                                                                • It is primarily composed of light gases such as hydrogen and helium, with extremely low atmospheric density.
                                                                                  • \n\t
                                                                                • Tropospheric clouds form in the troposphere, the lowest layer of Earth's atmosphere, not the exosphere.
                                                                                  • \n\t
                                                                                • The incorrect pairing arises because clouds, which require water vapor and relatively higher atmospheric density, cannot form in the exosphere due to its near-vacuum conditions.
                                                                                  • \n\t
                                                                                • Temperatures in the exosphere vary widely, and particles here are so sparse that they rarely collide, making cloud formation impossible.
                                                                                  • \n
                                                                                \n\n

                                                                                Additional Information

                                                                                \n\n
                                                                                  \n\t
                                                                                • Mesosphere - meteors burn\n\n\t
                                                                                    \n\t\t
                                                                                  • The mesosphere lies between the stratosphere and the thermosphere, extending from about 50 km to 85 km above Earth's surface.
                                                                                    • \n\t\t
                                                                                  • This layer is where most meteors burn upon entering Earth's atmosphere due to friction with air molecules.
                                                                                    • \n\t\t
                                                                                  • Temperatures in the mesosphere decrease with altitude, making it one of the coldest layers of the atmosphere.

                                                                                  The correct answer is Exosphere - tropospheric clouds.

                                                                                  \n\n

                                                                                  Key Points

                                                                                  \n\n
                                                                                    \n\t
                                                                                  • The exosphere is the outermost layer of Earth's atmosphere, located above the thermosphere.
                                                                                    • \n\t
                                                                                  • It is primarily composed of light gases such as hydrogen and helium, with extremely low atmospheric density.
                                                                                    • \n\t
                                                                                  • Tropospheric clouds form in the troposphere, the lowest layer of Earth's atmosphere, not the exosphere.
                                                                                    • \n\t
                                                                                  • The incorrect pairing arises because clouds, which require water vapor and relatively higher atmospheric density, cannot form in the exosphere due to its near-vacuum conditions.
                                                                                    • \n\t
                                                                                  • Temperatures in the exosphere vary widely, and particles here are so sparse that they rarely collide, making cloud formation impossible.
                                                                                    • \n

                                                                                  Additional Information

                                                                                  \n\n
                                                                                    \n\t
                                                                                  • Mesosphere - meteors burn\n\n\t
                                                                                      \n\t\t

                                                                                      • \n\t\t
                                                                                    • This layer is where most meteors burn upon entering Earth's atmosphere due to friction with air molecules.
                                                                                      • \n\t\t
                                                                                    • Temperatures in the mesosphere decrease with altitude, making it one of the coldest layers of the atmosphere.
                                                                                      • \n\t
                                                                                    \n\t
                                                                                    • \n\t
                                                                                  • Stratosphere - ozone layer\n\t
                                                                                      \n\t\t
                                                                                    • The stratosphere lies above the troposphere and extends from about 12 km to 50 km above Earth's surface.
                                                                                      • \n\t\t
                                                                                    • The ozone layer, which absorbs and scatters ultraviolet solar radiation, is located within this layer.
                                                                                      • \n\t\t
                                                                                    • Temperature increases with altitude in the stratosphere due to the absorption of UV radiation by the ozone layer.
                                                                                      • \n\t
                                                                                    \n\t
                                                                                    • \n\t
                                                                                  • Thermosphere - ISS orbit\n\t
                                                                                      \n\t\t
                                                                                    • The thermosphere is located above the mesosphere and extends from approximately 85 km to 600 km above Earth's surface.
                                                                                      • \n\t\t
                                                                                    • This layer is where the International Space Station (ISS) orbits, typically at an altitude of about 400 km.
                                                                                      • \n\t\t
                                                                                    • The thermosphere experiences extremely high temperatures due to the absorption of intense solar radiation, but the air density is so low that it would not feel hot to a human.

                                                                                    • \n\t
                                                                                  • यह मुख्य रूप से हाइड्रोजन और हीलियम जैसी हल्की गैसों से बना है, जिसमें वायुमंडलीय घनत्व बहुत कम होता है।
                                                                                    • \n\t

                                                                                      Shortcut Trick\n

                                                                                      When the sum of numbers under square roots is equal (7 + 2 = 9 and 6 + 3 = 9), the expression with the greater product is larger.

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      Product for first expression: 7 × 2 = 14

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      Product for second expression: 6 × 3 = 18

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      Since 14 < 18, we have √7 + √2 < √6 + √3.

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      ∴ The correct answer is (ii).

                                                                                      \n\n

                                                                                      Alternate Method

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      Given:

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      Expression 1 = √7 + √2

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      Expression 2 = √6 + √3

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      Formula Used:

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      (a + b)2 = a2 + b2 + 2ab

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      Calculation:

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      Let us compare the squares of both expressions.

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      ⇒ (√7 + √2)2 = (√7)2 + (√2)2 + 2 × √7 × √2

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      ⇒ (√7 + √2)2 = 7 + 2 + 2√14 = 9 + 2√14

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      ⇒ (√6 + √3)2 = (√6)2 + (√3)2 + 2 × √6 × √3

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      ⇒ (√6 + √3)2 = 6 + 3 + 2√18 = 9 + 2√18

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      Comparing the two results, we have the common term 9 in both.

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      We know that 14 < 18, so √14 < √18.

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      ⇒ 9 + 2√14 < 9 + 2√18

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      ⇒ (√7 + √2)2 < (√6 + √3)2

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      Taking square roots on both sides (since both sums are positive):

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      ⇒ √7 + √2 < √6 + √3

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      Thus, statement (ii) is correct.

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      ∴ The correct answer is (ii).

                                                                                      \n\n

                                                                                      Additional Information

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      Comparing Surds with Constant Sums

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      When the sum of the numbers inside the square roots is constant, simply check the product of the two numbers. The higher the product, the larger the value of the sum.

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      Comparing Surds with Constant Differences

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      If the difference between two surds is constant (e.g., √7 − √5 and √5 − √3), the expression with smaller numbers will always have a larger value.

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      Rationalization of Surds

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      Surds in the denominator are simplified by multiplying the numerator and denominator by the conjugate of the denominator, converting it into a rational number.

                                                                                      Shortcut Trick\n

                                                                                      जब वर्गमूलों के अंतर्गत संख्याओं का योग बराबर होता है (7 + 2 = 9 और 6 + 3 = 9), तो अधिक गुणनफल वाला व्यंजक बड़ा होता है।

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      पहले व्यंजक का गुणनफल: 7 × 2 = 14

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      दूसरे व्यंजक का गुणनफल: 6 × 3 = 18

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      चूँकि 14 < 18, तो हमारे पास √7 + √2 < √6 + √3 है।

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      ∴ सही उत्तर (ii) है।

                                                                                      \n\n

                                                                                      Alternate Method

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      दिया गया है:

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      व्यंजक 1 = √7 + √2

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      व्यंजक 2 = √6 + √3

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      प्रयुक्त सूत्र:

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      (a + b)2 = a2 + b2 + 2ab

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      गणना:

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      आइए हम दोनों व्यंजकों के वर्गों की तुलना करते हैं।

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      ⇒ (√7 + √2)2 = (√7)2 + (√2)2 + 2 × √7 × √2

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      ⇒ (√7 + √2)2 = 7 + 2 + 2√14 = 9 + 2√14

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      ⇒ (√6 + √3)2 = (√6)2 + (√3)2 + 2 × √6 × √3

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      ⇒ (√6 + √3)2 = 6 + 3 + 2√18 = 9 + 2√18

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      दोनों परिणामों की तुलना करने पर, हम देखते हैं कि दोनों में 9 एक उभयनिष्ठ पद है।

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      हम जानते हैं कि 14 < 18, इसलिए √14 < √18।

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      ⇒ 9 + 2√14 < 9 + 2√18

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      ⇒ (√7 + √2)2 < (√6 + √3)2

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      दोनों पक्षों का वर्गमूल लेने पर (चूंकि दोनों योग धनात्मक हैं):

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      ⇒ √7 + √2 < √6 + √3

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      इस प्रकार, कथन (ii) सही है।

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      ∴ सही उत्तर (ii) है।

                                                                                      \n\n

                                                                                      Additional Information

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      निश्चित योग के साथ करणी की तुलना

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      जब वर्गमूल के अंदर की संख्याओं का योग स्थिर होता है, तो बस दो संख्याओं के गुणनफल की जांच करें। गुणनफल जितना अधिक होगा, योग का मान उतना ही बड़ा होगा।

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      निश्चित अंतर के साथ करणी की तुलना

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      यदि दो करणी के बीच का अंतर स्थिर है (जैसे √7 − √5 और √5 − √3), तो छोटी संख्याओं वाले व्यंजक का मान हमेशा बड़ा होगा।

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      करणी का परिमेयकरण

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      हर में करणी को अंश और हर को हर के संयुग्म से गुणा करके सरल किया जाता है, इसे एक परिमेय संख्या में परिवर्तित किया जाता है।

                                                                                      மாறாத கூடுதலுடன் கூடிய கரணிகளை ஒப்பிடுதல்

                                                                                      வர்க்க மூலங்களுக்குள் உள்ள எண்களின் கூடுதல் மாறிலியாக இருக்கும்போது, இரு எண்களின் பெருக்கற்பலனைச் சரிபார்க்கவும். பெருக்கற்பலன் அதிகமாக இருந்தால், தொகையின் மதிப்பு பெரியதாக இருக்கும்.

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      Find two numbers whose sum is 9 and product is 20 ⇒ The numbers are 5 and 4.

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      The square root is directly the sum of their individual square roots: √5 + √4.

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      (a + b)2 = a2 + b2 + 2ab

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      Calculations:

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      ⇒ We need to express 9 + 4√5 as a perfect square to remove the outer root.

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      ⇒ Compare the irrational part (4√5) with the 2ab term of the identity: 2ab = 4√5 ⇒ ab = 2√5.

                                                                                      ⇒ By trial, possible values for a and b are 2 and √5.

                                                                                      ⇒ Since a2 + b2 = 9 perfectly matches the rational part, we can rewrite the expression:

                                                                                      Shortcut Trick\n

                                                                                      Total amount spent = SP of Laptop + SP of Mouse

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      Total amount spent = (40000 − 4000) + 1500 × (100 − 20)%

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      Total amount spent = 36000 + (1500 × 0.8)

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      Total amount spent = 36000 + 1200 = 37200

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      ∴ The correct answer is ₹37,200.

                                                                                      \n\n

                                                                                      Alternate Method

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      Given:

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      Price of the laptop = ₹40,000

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      Discount on the laptop = ₹4,000

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      Marked price of the mouse = ₹1,500

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      Discount percentage on the mouse = 20%

                                                                                      \n\n

                                                                                      Formula Used:

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      Selling Price (SP) = Marked Price (MP) − Discount Amount

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      Discount Amount = MP × (Discount % ÷ 100)

                                                                                      \n\n\n\n

                                                                                      Calculations:

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      ⇒ Selling price of laptop = 40,000 − 4,000 = ₹36,000

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      ⇒ Discount amount on mouse = 1,500 × (20 ÷ 100) = ₹300

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      ⇒ Selling price of mouse = 1,500 − 300 = ₹1,200

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      ⇒ Total amount spent = Selling price of laptop + Selling price of mouse

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      ⇒ Total amount spent = 36,000 + 1,200 = 37,200

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      ∴ The correct answer is ₹37,200.

                                                                                      \n\n

                                                                                      Additional Information

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      Marked Price (MP) vs Selling Price (SP)

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      Discount is always calculated on the Marked Price of an item. Formula: SP = MP × (1 − Discount%/100).

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      Successive Discount

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      When two discounts d1% and d2% are given consecutively, the effective discount is (d1 + d2 − (d1 × d2)/100)%.

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      Cost Price (CP) vs Selling Price (SP)

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      Profit or loss is always calculated on the Cost Price. Profit = SP − CP.

                                                                                      \nbd:T1587,\n

                                                                                      Shortcut Trick\n

                                                                                      खर्च की गई कुल राशि = लैपटॉप का SP + माउस का SP

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      खर्च की गई कुल राशि = (40000 − 4000) + 1500 x (100 − 20)%

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      खर्च की गई कुल राशि = 36000 + (1500 x 0.8)

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      खर्च की गई कुल राशि = 36000 + 1200 = 37200

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      ∴ सही उत्तर ₹37,200 है।

                                                                                      \n\n

                                                                                      प्रयुक्त सूत्र:

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      विक्रय मूल्य (SP) = अंकित मूल्य (MP) − छूट राशि

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      छूट राशि = MP x (छूट % ÷ 100)

                                                                                      \n\n Shortcut Trick\n

                                                                                      Total work = LCM(18, 24) = 72 units.

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      Efficiencies: A = 4 units/min, B = 3 units/min ⇒ Combined = 7 units/min.

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      Work done in 6 mins = 7 × 6 = 42 units ⇒ Remaining work = 72 − 42 = 30 units.

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      New combined efficiency = (4 ÷ 2) + (3 × 2) = 2 + 6 = 8 units/min.

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      Time for remaining work = 30 ÷ 8 = 3.75 minutes = 3 min 45 sec.

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      ∴ The correct answer is 3 min 45 sec.

                                                                                      \n\n

                                                                                      Alternate Method

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      Given:

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      Pipe A can fill the tank in 18 minutes.

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      Pipe B can fill the tank in 24 minutes.

                                                                                      \n\n \n \n \n \n \n \n \n \n \n \n \n \n \n \n \n \n \n \n \n
                                                                                      PipeTime (min)Total WorkEfficiency
                                                                                      A18724
                                                                                      B243
                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      Calculations:

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      ⇒ Initial combined efficiency of A and B = 4 + 3 = 7 units/min

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      ⇒ Work completed by both pipes in 6 minutes = 7 × 6 = 42 units

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      ⇒ Remaining work to be completed = 72 − 42 = 30 units

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      ⇒ New efficiency of Pipe A (halved) = 4 ÷ 2 = 2 units/min

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      ⇒ New efficiency of Pipe B (doubled) = 3 × 2 = 6 units/min

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      ⇒ New combined efficiency = 2 + 6 = 8 units/min

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      ⇒ Time required to complete the remaining work = 30 ÷ 8 = 15 / 4 minutes

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      ⇒ 15 / 4 minutes = 3 minutes + (3 / 4 × 60) seconds = 3 minutes 45 seconds

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      ∴ The correct answer is 3 min 45 sec.

                                                                                      \n\n

                                                                                      Additional Information

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      Pipes & Cisterns Concept

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      Filling pipes are considered to have a positive efficiency, while emptying pipes (leaks) have a negative efficiency. Total capacity is usually assumed as the LCM of individual timings.

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      Work and Efficiency Relation

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      Efficiency is inversely proportional to time. The foundational formula is Total Work = Efficiency × Time.

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      Unit Conversion

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      To convert a fractional part of a minute into exact seconds, simply multiply the fraction by 60 (e.g., 0.75 min = 0.75 × 60 = 45 sec).

                                                                                      Shortcut Trick\n

                                                                                      कुल कार्य = LCM(18, 24) = 72 इकाई।

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      दक्षता: A = 4 इकाई/मिनट, B = 3 इकाई/मिनट ⇒ संयुक्त = 7 इकाई/मिनट।

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      6 मिनट में किया गया कार्य = 7 x 6 = 42 इकाई ⇒ शेष कार्य = 72 − 42 = 30 इकाई।

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      नई संयुक्त दक्षता = (4 ÷ 2) + (3 x 2) = 2 + 6 = 8 इकाई/मिनट।

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      शेष कार्य के लिए समय = 30 ÷ 8 = 3.75 मिनट = 3 मिनट 45 सेकंड।

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      ∴ सही उत्तर 3 मिनट 45 सेकंड है।

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      Shortcut Trick\n

                                                                                      If x + (1/x) = −1, then a standard algebraic result is x3 = 1.

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      Reduce higher powers using this property: x4 = x3 × x = x.

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      ⇒ x4 + (1/x4) = x + (1/x) = −1

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      ⇒ 2(x2 + 1/x2) = 2[(x + 1/x)2 − 2] = 2[(−1)2 − 2] = −2

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      ⇒ Given expression value = −1 + (−2) = −3

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      ∴ The correct answer is −3.

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      Alternate Method

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      ⇒ x2 + (1/x2) = 1 − 2 = −1

                                                                                      Now, squaring this new equation on both sides:

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      ⇒ x4 + (1/x4) + 2 = 1

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      We need to find the value of: x4 + (1/x4) + 2x2 + (2/x2)

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      Shortcut Trick\n

                                                                                      10 जनवरी से 10 जुलाई तक का समय ठीक 6 महीने (1/2 वर्ष) है।

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      6% वार्षिक दर पर 6 महीने का साधारण ब्याज मूलधन का प्रभावी रूप से 3% है।

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      SI = 12000 का 3% = 3 × 120 = ₹360

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      कुल राशि = 12000 + 360 = ₹12360

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      ∴ सही उत्तर ₹12360 है।

                                                                                      \n\n

                                                                                      Alternate Method

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      दिया गया है:

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      मूलधन (P) = ₹12000

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      दर (R) = 6% प्रति वर्ष

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      समय (T) = 10 जनवरी 2024 से 10 जुलाई 2024 = 6 महीने = 1/2 वर्ष

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      प्रयुक्त सूत्र:

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      साधारण ब्याज (SI) = (P × R × T) ÷ 100

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      राशि (A) = P + SI

                                                                                      \n\n Shortcut Trick\n

                                                                                      Since the rate of interest and time period are the same, the ratio of Simple Interest to Principal remains constant.

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      Ratio for the first case = B ÷ A

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      Ratio for the second case = C ÷ B

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      Equating both ratios: B ÷ A = C ÷ B

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      By cross-multiplying, we get: B2 = AC

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      ∴ The correct answer is B2 = AC.

                                                                                      \n\n

                                                                                      Alternate Method

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      Given:

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      Simple interest on amount A = B

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      Simple interest on amount B = C

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      Rate of interest (R) and Time (T) are the same for both cases.

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      Formula Used:

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      Simple Interest (SI) = (P × R × T) ÷ 100

                                                                                      \n\n\n\n

                                                                                      Calculations:

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      ⇒ For the first case, the principal is A and simple interest is B.

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      ⇒ B = (A × R × T) ÷ 100

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      ⇒ (R × T) ÷ 100 = B ÷ A --- (Equation 1)

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      ⇒ For the second case, the principal is B and simple interest is C.

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      ⇒ C = (B × R × T) ÷ 100

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      ⇒ (R × T) ÷ 100 = C ÷ B --- (Equation 2)

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      ⇒ Equating Equation 1 and Equation 2, we get:

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      ⇒ B ÷ A = C ÷ B

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      ⇒ B × B = A × C

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      ⇒ B2 = AC

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      ∴ The correct answer is B2 = AC.

                                                                                      \n\n

                                                                                      Additional Information

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      Simple Interest (SI)

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      SI = (P × R × T) ÷ 100, where P = Principal, R = Rate %, and T = Time.

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      Compound Interest (CI)

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      Amount A = P × (1 + R ÷ 100)T and CI = A − P.

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      Difference between CI and SI

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      For 2 years, the difference is D = P × (R ÷ 100)2.

                                                                                      Shortcut Trick\n

                                                                                      चूंकि ब्याज दर और समय अवधि समान हैं, इसलिए साधारण ब्याज और मूलधन का अनुपात स्थिर रहता है।

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      पहले मामले के लिए अनुपात = B ÷ A

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      दूसरे मामले के लिए अनुपात = C ÷ B

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      दोनों अनुपातों को समान करने पर: B ÷ A = C ÷ B

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      वज्रगुणन करने पर, हमें प्राप्त होता है: B2 = AC

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      ∴ सही उत्तर B2 = AC है।

                                                                                      \n\n

                                                                                      Alternate Method

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      दिया गया है:

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      राशि A पर साधारण ब्याज = B

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      राशि B पर साधारण ब्याज = C

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      ब्याज की दर (R) और समय (T) दोनों मामलों के लिए समान हैं।

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      प्रयुक्त सूत्र:

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      साधारण ब्याज (SI) = (P × R × T) ÷ 100

                                                                                      \n\n\n\n

                                                                                      गणनाएँ:

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      ⇒ पहले मामले के लिए, मूलधन A है और साधारण ब्याज B है।

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      ⇒ B = (A × R × T) ÷ 100

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      ⇒ (R × T) ÷ 100 = B ÷ A --- (समीकरण 1)

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      ⇒ दूसरे मामले के लिए, मूलधन B है और साधारण ब्याज C है।

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      ⇒ C = (B × R × T) ÷ 100

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      ⇒ (R × T) ÷ 100 = C ÷ B --- (समीकरण 2)

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      ⇒ समीकरण 1 और समीकरण 2 को समान करने पर, हमें प्राप्त होता है:

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      ⇒ B ÷ A = C ÷ B

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      ⇒ B × B = A × C

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      ⇒ B2 = AC

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      ∴ सही उत्तर B2 = AC है।

                                                                                      \n\n

                                                                                      Additional Information

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      साधारण ब्याज (SI)

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      SI = (P × R × T) ÷ 100, जहाँ P = मूलधन, R = दर %, और T = समय।

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      चक्रवृद्धि ब्याज (CI)

                                                                                      CI और SI के बीच अंतर

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      Shortcut Trick\n

                                                                                      When the angle of elevation is 45°, the perpendicular and base of the right-angled triangle are equal.

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      Therefore, Height of the tower = Length of the shadow.

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      Length of shadow = 20 m.

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      ∴ The correct answer is 20 m.

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      Alternate Method

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      Given:

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      Height of the tower (AB) = 20 m

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      Angle of elevation of the sun (θ) = 45°

                                                                                      \n\n

                                                                                      Formula Used:

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      In a right-angled triangle, tan(θ) = Perpendicular ÷ Base

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      Calculation:

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      ⇒ Let the length of the shadow be BC.

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      ⇒ tan(45°) = AB ÷ BC

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      ⇒ 1 = 20 ÷ BC

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      ⇒ BC = 20 × 1

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      ⇒ BC = 20 m

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      ∴ The correct answer is 20 m.

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      Additional Information

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      Angle of Elevation vs Angle of Depression

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      The angle of elevation is formed when looking up from a horizontal line, while the angle of depression is formed when looking down. Both are numerically equal for the same line of sight due to alternate interior angles.

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      Standard Trigonometric Ratios

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      In height and distance problems, the most commonly used tangent values are tan(30°) = 1 ÷ √3, tan(45°) = 1, and tan(60°) = √3.

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      Complementary Elevation Angles

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      If a structure casts shadows of lengths a and b at two different times such that the respective angles of elevation are complementary (sum to 90°), the height of the structure is given by h = √(a × b).

                                                                                      Volume of a Full Sphere

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      The total volume of a complete sphere is given by V = (4/3) × π × r3. A hemisphere's volume is exactly half of this value.

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      Curved Surface Area (CSA)

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      The curved surface area of a hemisphere only accounts for the outer bowl part and is calculated as CSA = 2 × π × r2.

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      Total Surface Area (TSA)

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      The total surface area includes both the curved surface and the flat circular top surface. It is calculated as TSA = 3 × π × r2.
