                                                                                      Shortcut Trick\n

                                                                                      When a sphere is inscribed inside a cube, the ratio of the volume of the cube to the volume of the sphere is always 6 : π.

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      The ratio of the unoccupied volume to the total volume of the cube is (6 − π) : 6.

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      Percentage of unoccupied volume = [(6 − π) ÷ 6] × 100

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      Taking π ≈ 3.14: ⇒ [(6 − 3.14) ÷ 6] × 100 = (2.86 ÷ 6) × 100

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      ⇒ 47.666...% ≈ 47.67%

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      ∴ The correct answer is 47.67%.

                                                                                      \n\n

                                                                                      Alternate Method

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      Given:

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      A solid sphere is placed inside a cube such that it touches all six faces.

                                                                                      \n\n

                                                                                      Formula Used:

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      Volume of cube = a3 (where a is the side of the cube)

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      Volume of sphere = (4 ÷ 3) × π × r3 (where r is the radius of the sphere)

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      Calculations:

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      ⇒ Let the side of the cube be 'a'.

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      ⇒ Since the sphere touches all six faces, its diameter is strictly equal to the side length of the cube.

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      ⇒ Radius of the sphere (r) = a ÷ 2

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      ⇒ Volume of the cube = a3

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      ⇒ Volume of the sphere = (4 ÷ 3) × π × (a ÷ 2)3

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      ⇒ Volume of the sphere = (4 ÷ 3) × π × (a3 ÷ 8) = (π ÷ 6) × a3

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      ⇒ Volume not occupied by the sphere = a3 − (π ÷ 6) × a3 = a3 × (1 − π ÷ 6)

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      ⇒ Percentage of unoccupied volume = [a3 × (1 − π ÷ 6) ÷ a3] × 100

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      ⇒ Percentage = (1 − 3.14 ÷ 6) × 100

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      ⇒ Percentage = [(6 − 3.14) ÷ 6] × 100 = (2.86 ÷ 6) × 100

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      ⇒ Percentage = 286 ÷ 6 = 47.666... ≈ 47.67%

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      ∴ The correct answer is 47.67%.

                                                                                      \n\n

                                                                                      Additional Information

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      Cube Properties

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      For a cube of side 'a', the Total Surface Area = 6a2, Lateral Surface Area = 4a2, and the length of the longest body diagonal = a√3.

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      Sphere Properties

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      For a sphere of radius 'r', the Surface Area = 4πr2. The sphere holds the maximum possible volume among all 3D solid shapes for any given surface area.

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      Cube and Inscribed Cylinder

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      If a right circular cylinder is inscribed inside a cube of side 'a', its volume will be (π ÷ 4) × a3. The percentage of occupied volume by the cylinder is roughly 78.5%.

                                                                                      Shortcut Trick\n

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      अनावृत आयतन का घन के कुल आयतन से अनुपात (6 − π) : 6 है।

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      ⇒ 47.666...% ≈ 47.67%

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      एक ठोस गोले को एक घन के अंदर इस प्रकार रखा जाता है कि वह सभी छह फलकों को स्पर्श करता है।

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      ⇒ गोले द्वारा अनाधिकृत आयतन = a3 − (π ÷ 6) x a3 = a3 x (1 − π ÷ 6)

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      ⇒ अनावृत आयतन का प्रतिशत = [a3 x (1 − π ÷ 6) ÷ a3] x 100

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      ⇒ प्रतिशत = (1 − 3.14 ÷ 6) x 100

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      ⇒ प्रतिशत = [(6 − 3.14) ÷ 6] x 100 = (2.86 ÷ 6) x 100

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      ⇒ प्रतिशत = 286 ÷ 6 = 47.666... ≈ 47.67%

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      ∴ सही उत्तर 47.67% है।

                                                                                      Shortcut Trick\n

                                                                                      When a cone is cut parallel to its base, the volumes of the corresponding parts are proportional to the cubes of their heights.

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      Ratio of heights of top cone to total cone = 1 : 3.

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      Ratio of volumes of top cone to total cone = 13 : 33 = 1 : 27.

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      Volume of bottom part = Total volume − Top volume = 27 − 1 = 26 units.

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      Ratio of volumes of top part to bottom part = 1 : 26.

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      ∴ The correct answer is 1 : 26.

                                                                                      \n\n

                                                                                      Alternate Method

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      Given:

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      A conical bucket is cut at one-third of its height from the top.

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      Let total height of the cone be H and base radius be R.

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      Height of the top cone (h) = H / 3.

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      Formula Used:

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      Volume of cone = (1/3) × π × radius2 × height

                                                                                      \n\n Shortcut Trick\n

                                                                                      Let the sides of the rectangular prism be 4x, 5x, and 6x.

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      Volume = 4x × 5x × 6x = 120x3

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      Since the volume is 960 cm3, we have 120x3 = 960.

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      ⇒ x3 = 8 ⇒ x = 2

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      The longest side is 6x = 6 × 2 = 12 cm.

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      ∴ The correct answer is 12 cm.

                                                                                      \n\n

                                                                                      Alternate Method

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      Given:

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      The ratio of the sides of the rectangular prism = 4 : 5 : 6

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      Volume of the prism = 960 cm3

                                                                                      \n\n

                                                                                      Formula Used:

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      Volume of a rectangular prism = Length × Width × Height

                                                                                      \n\n\n\n

                                                                                      Calculations:

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      Let the exact dimensions of the sides be 4x, 5x, and 6x.

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      ⇒ Volume = 4x × 5x × 6x

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      ⇒ 120x3 = 960

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      ⇒ x3 = 960 ÷ 120

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      ⇒ x3 = 8

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      ⇒ x = 2

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      Now, finding the exact lengths of all three sides:

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      ⇒ First side = 4 × 2 = 8 cm

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      ⇒ Second side = 5 × 2 = 10 cm

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      ⇒ Third side = 6 × 2 = 12 cm

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      ⇒ Out of the three, the longest side is clearly 12 cm.

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      ∴ The correct answer is 12 cm.

                                                                                      \n\n

                                                                                      Additional Information

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      Total Surface Area (TSA)

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      The total area of all 6 faces of a cuboid is calculated using the formula: TSA = 2(lw + wh + hl).

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      Lateral Surface Area (LSA)

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      The area of the 4 side vertical faces (excluding the top and bottom) is calculated as: LSA = 2h(l + w).

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      Length of Diagonal

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      The longest distance between two opposite corners in a rectangular prism is given by the formula: Diagonal = √(l2 + w2 + h2).

                                                                                      Shortcut Trick\n

                                                                                      मान लीजिए आयताकार प्रिज्म की भुजाएँ 4x, 5x और 6x हैं।

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      आयतन = 4x × 5x × 6x = 120x3

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      चूँकि आयतन 960 सेमी3 है, हमारे पास 120x3 = 960 है।

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      ⇒ x3 = 8 ⇒ x = 2

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      सबसे लंबी भुजा 6x = 6 × 2 = 12 सेमी है।

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      ∴ सही उत्तर 12 सेमी है।

                                                                                      \n\n Shortcut Trick\n

                                                                                      When a cone is cut parallel to its base, the volumes of the corresponding parts are proportional to the cubes of their heights.

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      Ratio of heights of top cone to total cone = 1 : 3.

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      Ratio of volumes of top cone to total cone = 13 : 33 = 1 : 27.

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      Volume of bottom part = Total volume − Top volume = 27 − 1 = 26 units.

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      Ratio of volumes of top part to bottom part = 1 : 26.

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      ∴ The correct answer is 1 : 26.

                                                                                      \n\n

                                                                                      Alternate Method

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      Given:

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      A conical bucket is cut at one-third of its height from the top.

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      Let total height of the cone be H and base radius be R.

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      Height of the top cone (h) = H / 3.

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      Formula Used:

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      Volume of cone = (1/3) × π × radius2 × height

                                                                                      \n\n Shortcut Trick\n

                                                                                      जब एक शंकु को उसके आधार के समानांतर काटा जाता है, तो संबंधित भागों का आयतन उनकी ऊँचाई के घन के समानुपाती होता है।

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      शीर्ष शंकु और कुल शंकु की ऊँचाई का अनुपात = 1 : 3।

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      शीर्ष शंकु और कुल शंकु के आयतन का अनुपात = 13 : 33 = 1 : 27।

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      निचले भाग का आयतन = कुल आयतन - शीर्ष आयतन = 27 - 1 = 26 इकाई।

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      शीर्ष भाग और निचले भाग के आयतन का अनुपात = 1 : 26।

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      ∴ सही उत्तर 1 : 26 है।

                                                                                      \n\n

                                                                                      Alternate Method

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      दिया गया है:

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      एक शंक्वाकार बाल्टी को उसकी ऊँचाई के एक-तिहाई भाग पर ऊपर से काटा जाता है।

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      माना शंकु की कुल ऊँचाई H और आधार की त्रिज्या R है।

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      शीर्ष शंकु की ऊँचाई (h) = H / 3।

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      प्रयुक्त सूत्र:

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      शंकु का आयतन = (1/3) × π × त्रिज्या2 × ऊँचाई

                                                                                      \n\n\n\n

                                                                                      गणना:

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      ⇒ समरूप त्रिभुजों से, शीर्ष शंकु की त्रिज्या (r) = R / 3।

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      ⇒ शंकु का कुल आयतन (V) = (1/3) × π × R2 × H

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      ⇒ शीर्ष भाग का आयतन (V1) = (1/3) × π × r2 × h

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      ⇒ V1 = (1/3) × π × (R/3)2 × (H/3)

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      ⇒ V1 = (1/27) × [(1/3) × π × R2 × H]

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      ⇒ V1 = V / 27

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      ⇒ निचले भाग का आयतन (V2) = कुल आयतन - शीर्ष भाग का आयतन

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      ⇒ V2 = V - V/27

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      ⇒ V2 = 26V / 27

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      ⇒ शीर्ष भाग और निचले भाग का अनुपात = V1 : V2

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      ⇒ अनुपात = (V / 27) : (26V / 27)

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      ⇒ अनुपात = 1 : 26

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      ∴ सही उत्तर 1 : 26 है।

                                                                                      Additional Information

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      शंकु के छिन्नक का आयतन

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      जब एक शंकु को आधार के समानांतर काटा जाता है, तो निचला हिस्सा एक छिन्नक बनाता है। इसका आयतन V = (1/3) × π × h × (R2 + r2 + R × r) होता है, जहाँ h छिन्नक की ऊँचाई है।

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      Similar 3D Figures Property

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      k (ऊँचाई या त्रिज्या का अनुपात) के एक रैखिक माप कारक वाले किन्हीं दो समान ठोस आकृतियों के लिए, उनके पृष्ठीय क्षेत्र का अनुपात k2 होता है और उनके आयतन का अनुपात k3 होता है।

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      Relationship Between Cone and Cylinder

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      एक शंकु का आयतन एक बेलन के आयतन का ठीक एक-तिहाई होता है जिसकी आधार त्रिज्या और ऊँचाई समान होती है। Vशंकु = (1/3) × π × r2 × h।

                                                                                      \nce:Tfe4,\n

                                                                                      Shortcut Trick\n

                                                                                      Let the sides of the rectangular prism be 4x, 5x, and 6x.

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      Volume = 4x × 5x × 6x = 120x3

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      Since the volume is 960 cm3, we have 120x3 = 960.

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      ⇒ x3 = 8 ⇒ x = 2

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      The longest side is 6x = 6 × 2 = 12 cm.

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      ∴ The correct answer is 12 cm.

                                                                                      \n\n

                                                                                      Alternate Method

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      Given:

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      The ratio of the sides of the rectangular prism = 4 : 5 : 6

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      Volume of the prism = 960 cm3

                                                                                      \n\n

                                                                                      Formula Used:

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      Volume of a rectangular prism = Length × Width × Height

                                                                                      \n\n

                                                                                      Shortcut Trick\n

                                                                                      If x + 1/x = 2, then x = 1.

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      Here, tanA + cotA = 2 ⇒ tanA + 1/tanA = 2, so tanA = 1.

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      Since A ∈ (0, π/2), we get A = 45°.

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      Now, sinA + cosA = sin(45°) + cos(45°)

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      ⇒ 1/√2 + 1/√2 = 2/√2 = √2.

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      ∴ The correct answer is √2.

                                                                                      \n\n

                                                                                      Alternate Method

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      Given:

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      tanA + cotA = 2

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      A ∈ (0, π/2)

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      Formula Used:

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      tanA = sinA / cosA

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      cotA = cosA / sinA

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      (a + b)2 = a2 + b2 + 2ab

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      sin2A + cos2A = 1

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      Calculations:

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      ⇒ tanA + cotA = 2

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      ⇒ (sinA / cosA) + (cosA / sinA) = 2

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      ⇒ (sin2A + cos2A) / (sinA × cosA) = 2

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      ⇒ 1 / (sinA × cosA) = 2

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      ⇒ sinA × cosA = 1/2

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      We need to find the value of (sinA + cosA).

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      ⇒ Let (sinA + cosA) = x

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      Squaring both sides:

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      ⇒ (sinA + cosA)2 = x2

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      ⇒ sin2A + cos2A + 2 × sinA × cosA = x2

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      Substituting the known identities and values:

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      ⇒ 1 + 2 × (1/2) = x2

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      ⇒ 1 + 1 = x2

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      ⇒ x2 = 2

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      ⇒ x = √2 (Since A ∈ (0, π/2), both sinA and cosA are positive)

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      ∴ The correct answer is √2.

                                                                                      \n\n

                                                                                      Additional Information

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      Basic Trigonometric Identities

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      Three foundational identities are: sin2θ + cos2θ = 1, sec2θ − tan2θ = 1, and cosec2θ − cot2θ = 1.

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      Algebraic Reciprocal Property

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      If the sum of a number and its reciprocal is 2, i.e., x + 1/x = 2, then x must be equal to 1. This is frequently used in trigonometric equations involving tanθ and cotθ.

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      Signs in the First Quadrant

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      For any angle A ∈ (0, π/2), all six trigonometric ratios are positive. Thus, sinA + cosA must strictly be a positive value.

                                                                                      Shortcut Trick\n

                                                                                      यदि x + 1/x = 2, तो x = 1

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      यहाँ, tanA + cotA = 2 ⇒ tanA + 1/tanA = 2, तो tanA = 1.

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      चूंकि A ∈ (0, π/2), तो हमें A = 45° प्राप्त होता है।

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      अब, sinA + cosA = sin(45°) + cos(45°)

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      ⇒ 1/√2 + 1/√2 = 2/√2 = √2.

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      ∴ सही उत्तर √2 है।

                                                                                      \n\n

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      दिया गया है:

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      A ∈ (0, π/2)

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      प्रयुक्त सूत्र:

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      tanA = sinA / cosA

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      cotA = cosA / sinA

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      गणना:

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      ⇒ tanA + cotA = 2

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      ⇒ (sinA / cosA) + (cosA / sinA) = 2

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      ⇒ (sin2A + cos2A) / (sinA x cosA) = 2

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      ⇒ 1 / (sinA x cosA) = 2

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      ⇒ sinA x cosA = 1/2

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      हमें (sinA + cosA) का मान ज्ञात करना है।

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      ⇒ मान लीजिए (sinA + cosA) = x

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      दोनों पक्षों का वर्ग करने पर:

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      ⇒ (sinA + cosA)2 = x2

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      ⇒ sin2A + cos2A + 2 x sinA x cosA = x2

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      ज्ञात सर्वसमिकाओं और मानों को प्रतिस्थापित करने पर:

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      ⇒ 1 + 2 x (1/2) = x2

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      ⇒ 1 + 1 = x2

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      ⇒ x2 = 2

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      ⇒ x = √2 (चूंकि A ∈ (0, π/2), sinA और cosA दोनों धनात्मक हैं)

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      ∴ सही उत्तर √2 है।

                                                                                      Additional Information

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      मूल त्रिकोणमितीय सर्वसमिकाएँ

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      तीन मूलभूत सर्वसमिकाएँ हैं: sin2θ + cos2θ = 1, sec2θ − tan2θ = 1, और cosec2θ − cot2θ = 1.

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      बीजगणितीय व्युत्क्रम गुणधर्म

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      यदि किसी संख्या और उसके व्युत्क्रम का योग 2 है, अर्थात, x + 1/x = 2, तो x = 1 होना चाहिए। इसका उपयोग अक्सर tanθ और cotθ से जुड़े त्रिकोणमितीय समीकरणों में किया जाता है।

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      प्रथम चतुर्थांश में चिन्ह

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      Concept Used:

                                                                                      Calculation:

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      ⇒ 3 = 8 − 5 ⇒ 3 = 3 (Satisfies the equation)

                                                                                      Additional Information

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      Slope-Intercept Form

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      The equation of a straight line is commonly expressed as y = mx + c, where m is the slope (steepness) of the line and c is the y-intercept (where the line intersects the Y-axis).

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      Finding Axis Intercepts

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      To find the x-intercept, set y = 0 and solve for x. To find the y-intercept, set x = 0 and solve for y. For the line y = 4x − 5, the y-intercept is −5 and the x-intercept is 5/4.

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      Collinear Points

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      If three or more points lie exactly on the same straight line, they are mathematically defined as collinear. This is confirmed if they satisfy the same single linear equation.

                                                                                      Shortcut Trick\n

                                                                                      प्रत्येक विकल्प से निर्देशांक (x, y) को सीधे समीकरण y = 4x − 5 में प्रतिस्थापित कीजिए।

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      (1, −1) के लिए: −1 = 4(1) − 5 ⇒ −1 = −1 (सत्य)

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      (0, −5) के लिए: −5 = 4(0) − 5 ⇒ −5 = −5 (सत्य)

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      (2, 3) के लिए: 3 = 4(2) − 5 ⇒ 3 = 8 − 5 ⇒ 3 = 3 (सत्य)

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      चूंकि सभी बिंदु समीकरण को संतुष्ट करते हैं, इसलिए ग्राफ उन सभी से होकर गुजरता है।

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      ∴ सही उत्तर उपरोक्त सभी है।

                                                                                      \n\n

                                                                                      Alternate Method

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      दिया गया है:

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      ग्राफ का रैखिक समीकरण: y = 4x − 5

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      प्रयुक्त अवधारणा:

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      एक बिंदु (x, y) एक रेखा पर स्थित होता है यदि उसके निर्देशांक दिए गए रैखिक समीकरण को उसमें प्रतिस्थापित करने पर पूरी तरह से संतुष्ट करते हैं।

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      गणना:

                                                                                      \n\n

                                                                                      ⇒ बिंदु (1, −1) का परीक्षण:

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      ⇒ −1 = 4 x (1) − 5

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      ⇒ −1 = −1 (समीकरण को संतुष्ट करता है)

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      ⇒ बिंदु (0, −5) का परीक्षण:

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      ⇒ −5 = 4 x (0) − 5

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      ⇒ −5 = −5 (समीकरण को संतुष्ट करता है)

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      ⇒ बिंदु (2, 3) का परीक्षण:

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      ⇒ 3 = 4 x (2) − 5

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      ⇒ 3 = 8 − 5 ⇒ 3 = 3 (समीकरण को संतुष्ट करता है)

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      ⇒ चूंकि सभी दिए गए बिंदु समीकरण को सत्य बनाते हैं, रेखा का ग्राफ उन सभी से होकर गुजरता है।

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      ∴ सही उत्तर उपरोक्त सभी है।

                                                                                      \n\n

                                                                                      Additional Information

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      ढलान-अंतःखंड रूप

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      एक सीधी रेखा का समीकरण आमतौर पर y = mx + c के रूप में व्यक्त किया जाता है, जहाँ m रेखा का ढलान (ढलान) होता है और c y-अंतःखंड होता है (जहाँ रेखा Y-अक्ष को प्रतिच्छेद करती है)।

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      अक्ष अंतःखंड ढूँढना

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      x-अंतःखंड ज्ञात करने के लिए, y = 0 सेट करें और x के लिए हल करें। y-अंतःखंड ज्ञात करने के लिए, x = 0 सेट करें और y के लिए हल करें। रेखा y = 4x − 5 के लिए, y-अंतःखंड −5 है और x-अंतःखंड 5/4 है।

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      संरेख बिंदु

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      यदि तीन या अधिक बिंदु एक ही सीधी रेखा पर ठीक से स्थित होते हैं, तो उन्हें गणितीय रूप से संरेख के रूप में परिभाषित किया जाता है। इसकी पुष्टि तब होती है जब वे एक ही रैखिक समीकरण को संतुष्ट करते हैं।

                                                                                      \n Shortcut Trick\n

                                                                                      By basic geometric definitions, the term parallelogram explicitly means a quadrilateral with two pairs of parallel opposite sides.

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      A direct geometric consequence of opposite sides being parallel is that they must also be equal in length.

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      Statements defining only one parallel pair describe a trapezium, while perpendicular diagonals describe a rhombus.

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      ∴ The correct answer is Opposite sides are parallel and of equal length.

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      Alternate Method

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      Given:

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      Four statements describing the properties of quadrilaterals.

                                                                                      \n\n

                                                                                      Analysis of each option:

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      ⇒ Statement 1: Only one pair of opposite sides is parallel. This defines a Trapezium.

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      ⇒ Statement 2: All four interior angles are 90°. This defines a Rectangle or a Square.

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      ⇒ Statement 3: Opposite sides are parallel and of equal length. This is the exact definition and core property of a Parallelogram.

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      ⇒ Statement 4: The diagonals are perpendicular. This is a special property found in a Rhombus or a Square, but not in all standard parallelograms.

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      ⇒ Therefore, the only universally true statement for any general parallelogram is that its opposite sides are parallel and equal.

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      ∴ The correct answer is Opposite sides are parallel and of equal length.

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      Additional Information

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      Rhombus

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      A parallelogram in which all four sides are equal. The diagonals of a rhombus always bisect each other at exactly 90°.

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      Rectangle

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      A parallelogram where all four interior angles are exactly 90°. Its diagonals are equal in length and bisect each other.

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      Trapezium

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      A quadrilateral with strictly only one pair of parallel opposite sides. The parallel sides are called bases.

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      Square

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      A regular quadrilateral satisfying properties of both a rectangle and a rhombus. All sides are equal, all angles are 90°, and diagonals intersect at 90°.

                                                                                      मूल ज्यामितीय परिभाषाओं के अनुसार, समांतर चतुर्भुज शब्द का स्पष्ट अर्थ है एक चतुर्भुज जिसकी विपरीत भुजाओं के दो युग्म समानांतर होते हैं।

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      विपरीत भुजाओं के समानांतर होने का एक सीधा ज्यामितीय परिणाम यह है कि वे लंबाई में भी बराबर होनी चाहिए।

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      केवल एक समानांतर युग्म को परिभाषित करने वाले कथन समलंब को दर्शाते हैं, जबकि लंबवत विकर्ण समचतुर्भुज को दर्शाते हैं।

                                                                                      Alternate Method

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      Given:

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      Central angle of the first sector (θ1) = 60°

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      Central angle of the second sector (θ2) = π/3 radians

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      Formula Used:

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      Area of a sector (in degrees) = (θ ÷ 360°) × πr2

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      Area of a sector (in radians) = (1 ÷ 2) × r2θ

                                                                                      \n Shortcut Trick\n

                                                                                      Scale factor of the similar triangles = 39 ÷ 13 = 3.

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      Area of 1st triangle = 1/2 × 5 × √(132 − 52) = 1/2 × 5 × 12 = 30 cm2.

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      Ratio of areas = (Scale factor)2 = 32 = 9.

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      Area of 2nd triangle = 30 × 9 = 270 cm2.

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      ∴ The correct answer is 270 cm2.

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      Alternate Method

                                                                                      \n\n

                                                                                      Given:

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      Hypotenuse of first triangle = 13 cm

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      One leg of first triangle = 5 cm

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      Hypotenuse of second similar triangle = 39 cm

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      Formula Used:

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      Pythagoras Theorem: (Hypotenuse)2 = (Base)2 + (Height)2

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      Area of a Triangle = 1/2 × Base × Height

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      Calculations:

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      ⇒ Let the unknown leg of the first triangle be x.

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      ⇒ x2 + 52 = 132

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      ⇒ x2 + 25 = 169

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      ⇒ x2 = 144 ⇒ x = 12 cm

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      ⇒ The second triangle is similar to the first triangle.

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      ⇒ Ratio of their corresponding sides = 39 ÷ 13 = 3

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      ⇒ Therefore, the legs of the second triangle will also be 3 times the legs of the first triangle.

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      ⇒ Base of the second triangle = 5 × 3 = 15 cm

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      ⇒ Height of the second triangle = 12 × 3 = 36 cm

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      ⇒ Area of second triangle = 1/2 × 15 × 36

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      ⇒ Area of second triangle = 15 × 18 = 270 cm2

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      ∴ The correct answer is 270 cm2.

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      Additional Information

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      Properties of Similar Triangles

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      If two triangles are similar, the ratio of their corresponding sides, medians, altitudes, and perimeters is exactly equal to the scale factor (k).

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      Area Theorem for Similar Triangles

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      The ratio of the areas of two similar triangles is equal to the square of the ratio of their corresponding sides: Area1 ÷ Area2 = (Side1 ÷ Side2)2.

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      Pythagorean Triples

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      Memorizing common Pythagorean triples helps in fast calculations. Some common primitive triples are (3, 4, 5), (5, 12, 13), (8, 15, 17), and (7, 24, 25).

                                                                                      \n

                                                                                      समरूप त्रिभुजों का स्केल गुणक = 39 ÷ 13 = 3।

                                                                                      \n
